Method and limits

Reasoning uses think=true, context 16,384, output allowance 8,192. Prior direct runs use context 8,192, output allowance 2,048 and thinking disabled. Temperature 0 for all runs; other packaged model defaults retained. Model versions are checked against the direct runs. One attempt per response; no accuracy-driven retries. Warm-up excluded from timing. Reasoning traces are not included; the presence and size of thinking output are recorded.

The same 115 official responses exclude eight demonstration examples. No demonstration examples or target scores are sent to the models. Stimuli use verified text descriptions rather than images. General guidance was developed from these guides, and public examples may have appeared in training; this is not a fully independent benchmark. Grade 6 represents band 6–7, grade 8 represents 8–9. No grade 8–9 narrative task is present.
